# VICONIC ICON SYSTEM — Material Symbols INTEGRATION RULES
# Collection: Material Symbols
# Icon Prefix: `m9`

This file is an AI-readable ruleset for integrating icons from the **Material Symbols** collection of the **Viconic Icon System**.

---

## COLLECTION METADATA
- **Collection:** Material Symbols
- **Collection ID:** `material_symbols`
- **Icon Prefix:** `m9`
- **Author:** Google
- **License:** Apache 2.0
- **Total Icons:** 15380

> **IMPORTANT:** The icon prefix used in code is `m9`, NOT `material_symbols`.
> Always use the format `m9:icon-name` when referencing icons.

---

## INTEGRATION FORMATS

### Format 1: HTML (Web Component & CDN)

This method uses the Viconic Smart Loader CDN to render icons via custom Web Component `<viconic-icon>`.

**Step 1:** Include the Smart Loader script once in `<head>` or at the end of `<body>`:
```html
<script src="https://cdn.viconic.dev/js/copyicons-smart-loader.min.js"></script>
```

**Step 2:** Use the `<viconic-icon>` tag with `icon` attribute in the format `m9:[icon-name]`:
```html
<!-- Default usage (24px) -->
<viconic-icon icon="m9:10k"></viconic-icon>

<!-- Custom size and color via inline CSS -->
<viconic-icon icon="m9:10k" style="font-size: 32px; color: #3b82f6;"></viconic-icon>
```

---

### Format 2: JSX / TSX (React)

For React, Next.js, or Vite projects using TypeScript/JSX:

#### Option A: Internal project component
If the project has `src/components/ViconicIcon`, import from there:
```tsx
import ViconicIcon from '@/components/ViconicIcon';

function MyComponent() {
  return (
    <div>
      {/* Default usage */}
      <ViconicIcon name="m9:10k" />

      {/* Custom size and color */}
      <ViconicIcon name="m9:10k" size="2rem" color="currentColor" />
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### Option B: NPM package `viconic-react-icons`
Install:
```bash
npm install viconic-react-icons
```
Usage:
```tsx
import { ViconicIcon } from 'viconic-react-icons';

function MyComponent() {
  return (
    <div>
      <ViconicIcon name="m9:10k" />
      <ViconicIcon name="m9:10k" style={{ fontSize: '32px', color: '#10b981' }} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

---

### Format 3: SVG (Static)

No JavaScript required. Best for SSR, performance-critical, or offline use.

#### Option A: Inline SVG
Copy the raw SVG markup directly into your HTML/TSX. This allows full CSS styling control:
```html
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" height="24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
  <!-- Paste the SVG path data for your chosen icon here -->
  <path stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" d="..." />
</svg>
```
*To get the actual SVG content, fetch it from:*
`https://cdn.viconic.dev/icons/material_symbols/[icon-name].svg`

#### Option B: `<img>` tag pointing to CDN
```html
<img 
  src="https://cdn.viconic.dev/icons/material_symbols/10k.svg" 
  alt="10k" 
  width="24" 
  height="24" 
/>
```

---

## STRICT CODING RULES (ANTI-PATTERNS)
- ❌ **DO NOT** hardcode hex colors (`stroke="#000"`, `fill="#fff"`) in SVG. Always use `currentColor`.
- ❌ **DO NOT** include the CDN `<script>` tag more than once per HTML document.
- ❌ **DO NOT** omit accessibility attributes. Use `aria-hidden="true"` for decorative icons, or wrap in `<button aria-label="...">` for interactive ones.
- ❌ **DO NOT** use the collection ID `material_symbols` as the icon prefix. The correct prefix is `m9`.
- ❌ **DO NOT** invent icon names not listed in this file. Only use names from the list below.

---
